Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the riemann_surfaces object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 913. The moduli space has dimension at most 400 with 4 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the riemann_surfaces decomposition. By minimality, the element a^913 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 400-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
